Scripts and programs don't belong in an IG's repository: they arrive with every pull, and people run them out of habit, so anyone who can change the repository can run code on the computer of everyone who builds the IG. Remove them from the repository (people who want them can get them from their source, and keep them out of the IG's repository). If you accept these as they are, you can say so now by running the IG Publisher with '-allow-committed-scripts' (recorded in /home/runner/.fhir/fhir-settings.json, for this IG), and local builds will go on then (but not the ci-build).
